             <div id="consensus-explanation" class="explanation mb-0">
                 <div class="pb-3">MarketBeat calculates consensus analyst ratings for stocks using the most recent rating from each Wall Street analyst that has rated a stock within the last twelve months. Each analyst's rating is normalized to a standardized rating score of 1 (sell), 2 (hold), 3 (buy) or 4 (strong buy). Analyst consensus ratings scores are calculated using the mean average of the number of normalized sell, hold, buy and strong buy ratings from Wall Street analysts. Each stock's consensus analyst rating is derived from its calculated consensus ratings score (0 to .5 = Strong Sell, .5 to 1 = Sell, 1 to 1.5 = Reduce, 1.5 to 2.5 = Hold, 2.5 to 3.0 = Moderate Buy, 3.0 to 3.5 = Buy, >3.5 = Strong Buy). MarketBeat's consensus price targets are a mean average of the most recent available price targets set by each analyst that has set a price target for the stock in the last twelve months.  MarketBeat's consensus ratings and consensus price targets may differ from those calculated by other firms due to differences in methodology and available data.</div>

            <div class="pr-4 position-relative"><h2 class="m-0 mb-2 h3 d-inline-block">NVDA Analyst Recommendations By Month</h2><button id="rating-question-mark" class="clear-button position-absolute question-toggle c-white" style="top:0;right:0;" type="button"><span class="fa-regular fa-circle-question" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="sr-only">Analyst Ratings Chart Description</span></button></div><div class="border rounded mb-3"><div id="rating-explanation" class="explanation"><div class="p-3">The chart below shows how a company's ratings by analysts have changed over time. Each bar represents the previous year of ratings for that month. Within each bar, the sell ratings are shown in red, the hold ratings are shown in yellow, the buy ratings are shown in green, and the strong buy ratings are shown in dark green.</div></div><a href="#ratings-table" class="sr-only sr-only-focusable">Skip Chart & View Analyst Rating History<span class="fa-regular fa-angle-right ml-3" aria-hidden="true"></span></a><div class="dv-chart-wrapper px-2"><div id="analystRatingChart" class="dv-chart" style="height:350px"></div></div></div>

    <div id="cphPrimaryContent_cphTabContent_pnlInsights" class="mb-4">
		
        <div class="section-heading position-relative pr-3">
            <h2 id="insights">Should I Buy NVIDIA Stock? NVDA Pros and Cons Explained</h2>
            <span class="inline-moreinfo moreinfo float-right"><span class="fa-regular fa-circle-question" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="tooltiptext" style="width:250px;">These pros and cons were generated based on recent news and financial data from MarketBeat in order to provide readers with the fastest and most accurate insights. They were last updated on Monday, September 7, 2026. Please send any questions or comments about these NVIDIA pros and cons to contact@marketbeat.com.</span></span>
        </div>
        <div class="row ai-pros-cons-row">
    <div class="col-xl-6 ai-pros mb-2"><div class="inner-border">
        <h3 style="padding-right:88px;">NVIDIA <br/> Bull Case</h3><img style="width:77px" src="/images/green-bull.svg" alt="green Wall Street bull icon" width="77" height="45" />
        <p>Here are some ways that investors could benefit from investing in NVidia: </p>
        <ul>
            <li>NVidia's acquisition of Hugging Face, a platform hosting millions of models used by over 18 million developers, significantly strengthens the company's software moat by deepening CUDA adoption and positioning NVidia as a comprehensive AI platform provider rather than solely a chip supplier.</li>
            <li>The company reported exceptional financial performance in its most recent quarter with revenue of $96.22 billion, which exceeded analyst expectations and represented a 105.9% year-over-year increase, alongside a net margin of 63.66% and earnings per share of $2.22 that beat consensus estimates.</li>
            <li>With the current stock price at $230.36, NVidia trades at a price-to-earnings ratio of 29.12, which is notably lower than the consensus target price of $324.83, suggesting potential upside for investors given the company's strong growth trajectory and dominant market position.</li>
            <li>NVidia is expanding its addressable market beyond data centers by launching consumer AI PCs powered by its RTX Spark chip in partnership with Lenovo, Acer, and HP, which allows for local AI processing and reduces reliance on cloud infrastructure while opening new revenue channels.</li>
            <li>The board has authorized an $80 billion stock repurchase program, signaling management's confidence in the company's valuation and providing a mechanism to return capital to shareholders, which can support stock price stability and enhance shareholder value.</li>
        </ul></div>
    </div>
    <div class="col-xl-6 ai-cons mb-2"><div class="inner-border">
        <h3 style="padding-right:88px;">NVIDIA <br/> Bear Case</h3><img style="width:77px" src="/images/red-bear.svg" alt="red Wall Street bear icon" width="77" height="48" />
        <p>Investors should be bearish about investing in NVidia for these reasons:</p>
        <ul>
            <li>Significant insider selling activity has been observed, with Director Mark A. Stevens selling over 1.5 million shares and EVP Timothy S. Teter selling 30,000 shares in recent months, which may signal that company insiders are taking advantage of high valuations to reduce their personal exposure.</li>
            <li>Competitive pressure from companies like AMD, Broadcom, and Intel, who are actively pursuing AI and server opportunities, poses a risk to NVidia's market dominance, particularly as Broadcom's custom AI accelerators gain traction for inference tasks and hyperscalers seek diverse chip solutions.</li>
            <li>Rising costs for memory and optical networking components could pressure NVidia's gross margins, even if demand exceeds supply, as the company faces increased input costs that may erode its historically high profitability levels.</li>
            <li>The company's high beta of 2.22 indicates that NVidia's stock is significantly more volatile than the overall market, meaning investors may experience larger price swings and higher risk during market downturns or periods of uncertainty in the AI sector.</li>
            <li>Growing opposition to AI data centers and potential regulatory or community backlash against the massive infrastructure buildout required for AI could constrain future expansion and create new operational or reputational risks for NVidia's core business model.</li>
        </ul></div>
    </div>
</div>
    
	</div>

        function loadMoreDescription(Prefix, Symbol) {

            var url = "/Scripts/ReadMore.ashx?Prefix=" + Prefix + "&Symbol=" + Symbol;

                type: "POST",
                url: url,

    <script>
        function CreateOnModalSubmit(token) {
            $("#btnCreateOnModal").click();
            
        }

        function LoginOnModalSubmit() {
            CheckLoginOnModal("txtLoginOnModalEmail", "txtLoginOnModalPassword", "loginOnModalWarning", "btnLoginOnModal");
            return false;
        }

        function SendMagicLinkOnModal(token) {
            var notice = document.getElementById('loginOnModalWarning');
            var email = document.getElementById('txtLoginOnModalEmail').value;
            if (email == "") {
                notice.innerHTML = "<span class='c-red'>Please enter your email address.</span>";
                return;
            }
            document.getElementById('btnMagicLinkOnModal').classList.add("active-loading");
            var returnUrl = window.location.pathname + window.location.search;
            console.log('[magiclink] sending', { tokenLength: (token || '').length, returnUrlLength: returnUrl.length });

            // POST, not GET: the reCAPTCHA token alone can blow past the 2048-char query string limit
            $.ajax({
                type: 'POST',
                url: '/scripts/modal/EmailChecker.ashx',
                data: {
                    command: 'magiclink',
                    email: email,
                    returnUrl: returnUrl,
                    recaptchaToken: token
                },
                success: function (data) {
                    var btn = document.getElementById('btnMagicLinkOnModal');
                    btn.classList.remove("active-loading");
                    console.log('[magiclink] response', data);
                    if (data.indexOf('c-red') > -1) {
                        notice.innerHTML = data;
                        // v2 invisible tokens are single-use â€” reset or the next click yields nothing
                        if (typeof grecaptcha !== 'undefined') { grecaptcha.reset(); }
                    } else {
                        notice.innerHTML = "<span class='c-green'>" + data + "</span>";
                        btn.disabled = true;
                        btn.classList.remove('btn-secondary');
                        btn.classList.add('btn-disabled');
                    }
                },
                error: function (xhr, textStatus, errorThrown) {
                    document.getElementById('btnMagicLinkOnModal').classList.remove("active-loading");
                    console.error('[magiclink] failed', {
                        status: xhr.status,               // 404 = IIS rejected the request (404.15 = query string too long)
                        statusText: xhr.statusText,
                        textStatus: textStatus,
                        errorThrown: errorThrown,
                        tokenLength: (token || '').length,
                        response: (xhr.responseText || '').substring(0, 1000)
                    });
                    notice.innerHTML = "<span class='c-red'>Something went wrong sending your login link. Please try again.</span>";
                    if (typeof grecaptcha !== 'undefined') { grecaptcha.reset(); }
                }
            });
        }

        // Toggle modal magic link button style based on email input
        document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
            if (e.target.id === 'txtLoginOnModalEmail') {
                var btn = document.getElementById('btnMagicLinkOnModal');
                if (btn) {
                    var hasEmail = e.target.value.trim() !== '';
                    btn.classList.toggle('btn-disabled', !hasEmail);
                    btn.classList.toggle('btn-secondary', hasEmail);
                }
            }
        });

        function setPremiumPageWording() {
            $('.free').addClass("d-none");
        }
    </script>

    <script type="text/javascript">

        (function () {
            const second = 1000,
                minute = second * 60,
                hour = minute * 60,
                day = hour * 24;

            let today = new Date(),
                eventDate = new Date(Date.UTC(2023, 06, 29, 05, 59, 00, 00)); // must be 5 hours 59 minutes after you want it to end because of time zones and the month is zero indexed

            //end

            const countDown = new Date(eventDate).getTime(),
                x = setInterval(function () {

                    if (document.getElementById("countdown") != null) {
                        const now = new Date().getTime(),
                            distance = countDown - now;

                        document.getElementById("days").innerText = Math.floor(distance / (day)),
                            document.getElementById("hours").innerText = Math.floor((distance % (day)) / (hour)),
                            document.getElementById("minutes").innerText = Math.floor((distance % (hour)) / (minute)),
                            document.getElementById("seconds").innerText = Math.floor((distance % (minute)) / second);

                        //do something later when date is reached
                        if (distance <= 0) {
                            document.getElementById("days").innerText = 0,
                                document.getElementById("hours").innerText = 0,
                                document.getElementById("minutes").innerText = 0,
                                document.getElementById("seconds").innerText = 0;
                            clearInterval(x);
                        }
                        //seconds

                    }
                }, 200)
        }());



        (function () {
            if (document.getElementById("countdownIndividual") != null) {

                const second = 1000,
                    minute = second * 60,
                    hour = minute * 60,
                    day = hour * 24;


                var daysToOffer = 2;
                let expirationDate = new Date(new Date().getTime() + (daysToOffer * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));

                if (getCookie("HIExpiration")) {
                    expirationDate = new Date(getCookie("HIExpiration"));
                }
                else {
                    setCookie("HIExpiration", expirationDate.toJSON(), 16)
                }

                //end
                const countDown = new Date(expirationDate).getTime(),
                    x = setInterval(function () {


                        const now = new Date().getTime(),
                            distance = countDown - now;

                        document.getElementById("days").innerText = Math.floor(distance / (day)),
                            document.getElementById("hours").innerText = Math.floor((distance % (day)) / (hour)),
                            document.getElementById("minutes").innerText = Math.floor((distance % (hour)) / (minute)),
                            document.getElementById("seconds").innerText = Math.floor((distance % (minute)) / second);

                        //do something later when date is reached
                        if (distance <= 0) {
                            document.getElementById("days").innerText = 0,
                                document.getElementById("hours").innerText = 0,
                                document.getElementById("minutes").innerText = 0,
                                document.getElementById("seconds").innerText = 0;
                            clearInterval(x);
                        }
                        //seconds


                    }, 200)
            }
        }());
    </script>



</body>
</html>
